### [1] [Highly improved staggered quarks on anisotropic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28543)
*Alexei Bazavov,Yannis Trimis,Johannes Heinrich Weber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了aHISQ费米子作用量在纯规范系综上的各向异性调谐，比较了不同梯度流方案对规范各向异性的调谐效果，并对比了naive staggered与aHISQ在费米子各向异性调谐上的差异；进一步分析了两种作用量下staggered π介子味质量劈裂随各向异性的依赖关系，提出了一个经验模型刻画aHISQ谱的主要特征，并指出naive staggered与aHISQ在味谱各向异性行为上存在定性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升各向异性格点QCD模拟的精度与效率，需精确调谐aHISQ作用量的规范与费米子各向异性参数，尤其在高各向异性（ξ=1–8）下确保物理谱的正确再现。

Method: 采用多种梯度流方案调谐纯规范系综的规范各向异性；对比naive staggered与aHISQ的费米子各向异性调谐策略；系统测量不同各向异性下的staggered pion taste mass splittings；基于数据构建经验模型拟合aHISQ味谱结构。

Result: 发现不同梯度流方案在规范各向异性调谐中表现各异，其中某类方案更适用于高各向异性模拟；aHISQ费米子各向异性调谐比naive staggered更稳健；aHISQ的味质量劈裂随各向异性演化呈现独特模式，可被所提经验模型良好描述；naive staggered与aHISQ的味谱对各向异性的响应存在本质区别。

Conclusion: aHISQ作用量在高各向异性下展现出更优的谱行为与调谐可控性，所提出的梯度流调谐方案与经验谱模型为未来高精度各向异性格点计算提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: We present a study of tuning of the anisotropic highly improved staggered quark (aHISQ) action on pure gauge ensembles with the renormalized anisotropy ranging from 1 to 8. We discuss multiple gradient flow schemes for tuning the gauge anisotropy and comment on what scheme may be optimal for anisotropic simulations. Next, we compare tuning of the fermion anisotropy for the naive staggered and aHISQ actions. Finally, we study the dependence of the staggered pion taste mass splittings on anisotropy for the two actions and develop an empirical model that captures the main features of the aHISQ spectrum. We observe qualitatively different behavior of the naive and aHISQ taste spectrum with anisotropy.

</details>


### [2] [Complete Access to Leading-Twist $Λ$-Baryon Light-Cone Distribution Amplitudes from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29597)
*Mu-Hua Zhang,Haoyang Bai,Min-Huan Chu,Jun Hua,Xiangdong Ji,Xiangyu Jiang,Jian Liang,Cai-Dian Lü,Andreas Schäfer,Wei Wang,Yi-Bo Yang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Jia-Lu Zhang,Qi-An Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次通过格点QCD完整确定了Λ重子的领扭度轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs），作为价夸克动量分数的二维函数；采用大动量有效理论（LaMET）将轻锥振幅与 boosted Λ 重子的等时非局域三夸克矩阵元关联；通过连续极限、物理π质量及无穷动量外推，并结合混合重整化、大λ外推与微扰匹配，获得三个领扭度LCDAs（V, A, T）；用此格点结果替代渐近形式后，在微扰能标下Λ电磁形状因子出现~10%修正，表明完整二维LCDAs对重子精确唯象学不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重子结构研究常依赖LCDAs的渐近形式或低阶矩，但其不足以刻画高精度唯象需求；需从第一性原理出发，直接计算完整的x-依赖二维LCDAs以提升重子过程的理论预测精度。

Method: 基于大动量有效理论（LaMET），在格点QCD中构造并计算 boosted Λ 重子的等时非局域三夸克算符矩阵元；系统实施 continuum limit、physical pion mass extrapolation、infinite-momentum limit；采用 hybrid renormalization、large-λ extrapolation 和 perturbative matching 到 light-cone scheme；最终提取三维领扭度LCDAs V(x₁,x₂), A(x₁,x₂), T(x₁,x₂)。

Result: 首次获得第一性原理定义的、全二维x₁-x₂依赖的Λ重子领扭度LCDAs V, A, T；数值显示其显著偏离渐近形式；代入Λ电磁形状因子计算，导致~10%的修正；确立了多维重子结构的格点基准。

Conclusion: 完整二维LCDAs是重子精密唯象学的必要输入；本工作与配套论文[1]共同建立了首个从第一性原理出发的多维重子轻锥结构框架，标志着格点QCD进入重子内部多维动量空间解析的新阶段。

Abstract: We report the first complete lattice-QCD determination of the leading-twist light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) of the $Λ$ baryon, obtained as full two-dimensional functions of the valence-quark momentum fractions. The calculation employs large-momentum effective theory to relate the light-cone amplitudes to equal-time nonlocal three-quark matrix elements of boosted $Λ$ baryons. Controlled physical extrapolations to the continuum, physical pion mass, and infinite momentum, together with hybrid renormalization, large-$λ$ extrapolation, and perturbative matching, yield the three leading-twist LCDAs $V$, $A$, and $T$. Using the lattice-determined LCDAs in place of the asymptotic form, we find an $\mathcal{O}(10\%)$ shift in the $Λ$ electromagnetic form factor at perturbative scales, demonstrating that the full two-dimensional LCDAs, rather than only their asymptotic shapes or lowest moments, are required for precision baryonic phenomenology. This work, together with the companion paper [1] detailing the baryon-LaMET framework, provides the first complete multi-dimensional $x$-dependent baryon LCDAs from first principles and establishes a benchmark for lattice access to multi-dimensional baryon structure.

</details>


### [3] [The topological susceptibility slope $χ^\prime$ in the large-$N$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29885)
*Claudio Bonanno*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在大N极限下通过非微扰格点计算确定了杨-米尔斯理论的拓扑磁化率斜率χ′，提出了一种避免大N下拓扑冻结的新算法及格点上可靠提取χ′的新方法，结果对Shore-Veneziano公式描述质子自旋具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定杨-米尔斯理论中拓扑磁化率斜率χ′，该量在动量展开中对应O(p²)项，对强相互作用的理论结构（如η′质量、U(1)ₐ问题）及现象学（如质子自旋分解）具有关键意义。

Method: 采用新型格点算法克服大N极限下细格点上的拓扑冻结问题，并发展出一种基于拓扑电荷密度两点关联函数动量展开的格点可重整化方法来提取χ′。

Result: 首次获得大N极限下非微扰格点计算的χ′数值结果，验证了其有限且可外推至连续极限的性质，为Shore-Veneziano公式提供首个第一性原理支持。

Conclusion: χ′是一个普适、可计算的非微扰量，其格点测定证实了大N下拓扑结构的可控性，为连接QCD拓扑与实验可观测量（如质子自旋）建立了严格桥梁。

Abstract: This paper presents the first non-perturbative lattice determination of the Yang--Mills topological susceptibility slope $χ^\prime$ in the large-$N$ limit. This quantity represents the $\mathcal{O}(p^2)$ term of the momentum expansion of the topological charge density two-point correlator, and has important theoretical and phenomenological implications for strong interactions. This calculation is based on a novel algorithm that avoids topological freezing at large $N$ on fine lattices, and on a novel method to reliably compute $χ^\prime$ on the lattice. The results of this study are relevant for the description of the proton spin in deep inelastic scattering experiments via the Shore--Veneziano formula.

</details>


### [4] [Isospin-Driven Splitting of Chemical Potentials in Isobar Collisions from Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30164)
*Heng-Tong Ding,Jin-Biao Gu,Arpith Kumar,Jia Ni*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理(2+1)味格点QCD计算，在QCD相变交叉区研究了强磁场对Ru-Ru与Zr-Zr等质量同位素系统中守恒荷化学势劈裂的影响，发现电荷化学势劈裂Δμ_Q主导且为负，奇异化学势劈裂Δμ_S为正；磁场仅引起劈裂比的中等变化，但μ_Q/μ_B对磁场的归一化响应在格点QCD中显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论重离子碰撞中产生的强磁场可能改变守恒荷涨落及其化学势，而Ru-Zr等质量同位素实验（如STAR）提供了探测磁场效应的理想平台，亟需理论框架将核子同位素差异映射至化学势劈裂，并量化磁场影响。

Method: 采用(2+1)味格点QCD模拟，在赝临界温度线T_pc(eB)上计算零场与非零场下Ru-Ru与Zr-Zr系统的同位旋驱动化学势劈裂Δμ_Q、Δμ_S、Δμ_B；结合奇异数守恒与电荷-重子比r=n_Q/n_B约束，构建劈裂比Δμ_Q/Δμ_B、Δμ_S/Δμ_B、Δμ_S/Δμ_Q关于μ_B(r_Ru)/Δμ_B的映射；辅以HRG模型与实验可测代理观测量分析。

Result: 零磁场下，劈裂比量级与STAR贝叶斯抽取结果一致，Δμ_Q<0、Δμ_S>0，电荷扇区主导；非零磁场下劈裂比仅呈中等eB依赖，但μ_Q/μ_B的归一化磁场响应在格点QCD中显著增强；HRG结果与实验代理观测量（含运动学截断）被给出以连接实验。

Conclusion: 格点QCD证实Ru-Zr同位素化学势劈裂主要由同位旋差异驱动，电荷化学势劈裂占优且符号确定；强磁场不显著改变劈裂比，但显著调制μ_Q/μ_B对磁场的响应，为通过守恒荷涨落探测QCD介质中的电磁响应提供了新理论依据。

Abstract: Strong magnetic fields produced in relativistic heavy-ion collisions can modify fluctuations of conserved charges and, consequently, their associated chemical potentials. We present first-principles $(2+1)$-flavor lattice-QCD results for isospin-driven splittings of conserved-charge chemical potentials between the isobar systems $^{96}_{44}\mathrm{Ru}+^{96}_{44}\mathrm{Ru}$ and $^{96}_{40}\mathrm{Zr}+^{96}_{40}\mathrm{Zr}$ in the QCD crossover region, both at vanishing and nonzero magnetic fields along the pseudo-critical line $T_{pc}(eB)$. We outline a framework that, under strangeness neutrality and charge-to-baryon ratio $r\equiv n_{\rm Q}/n_{\rm B}$, maps the isospin difference between two nuclei, as encoded in $r_{\rm Zr}$ and $r_{\rm Ru}$, onto splitting ratios $Δμ_{\rm Q}/Δμ_{\rm B}$, $Δμ_{\rm S}/Δμ_{\rm B}$, and $Δμ_{\rm S}/Δμ_{\rm Q}$ as functions of $μ_{\rm B}(r_{\rm Ru})/Δμ_{\rm B}$. Using continuum-estimated lattice results for the leading-order coefficients $q_1\equiv(μ_{\rm Q}/μ_{\rm B})_{\rm LO}$ and $s_1\equiv(μ_{\rm S}/μ_{\rm B})_{\rm LO}$, we find that, at vanishing magnetic field, the splitting ratios are of similar magnitude to recent Bayesian extractions from STAR isobar data and yield $Δμ_{\rm Q}<0$ and $Δμ_{\rm S}>0$, with the electric-charge sector dominating. At nonzero magnetic fields, the splitting ratios show only moderate $eB$ dependence. We therefore further examine Ru--Zr differences in the normalized magnetic-field response of chemical-potential ratios, particularly those involving $μ_{\rm Q}/μ_{\rm B}$, which display a pronounced enhancement in lattice QCD. We also present hadron resonance gas (HRG) results and experimentally motivated proxy observables with kinematic cuts to facilitate contact with experiment.

</details>


### [5] [Baryon Light-Cone Distribution Amplitudes from Lattice QCD: Formalism, Renormalization, Extrapolation, and Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30387)
*Mu-Hua Zhang,Haoyang Bai,Min-Huan Chu,Jun Hua,Xiangdong Ji,Xiangyu Jiang,Jian Liang,Cai-Dian Lü,Andreas Schäfer,Wei Wang,Yi-Bo Yang,Jian-Hui Zhang,Jia-Lu Zhang,Qi-An Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于大动量有效理论（LaMET）的系统性框架，用于从格点QCD第一性原理计算重子轻锥分布振幅（LCDAs），涵盖从等时三夸克准分布振幅到物理LCDAs的完整路径，并以Λ重子A-结构为例进行了全流程验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重子LCDAs是二维动量分数依赖的多维对象，其第一性原理确定比介子LCDAs困难得多；现有方法缺乏系统性的LaMET框架来处理其非局域性、对称性与重整化问题。

Method: 构建LaMET框架：定义V/A/T型准DA，分析其自旋-味与坐标空间对称性；提出(z₁,z₂)平面上的混合重整化方案；发展基于欧氏关联函数大距离渐近行为的大λ外推策略；导出混合重整化方案下一阶圈图LaMET匹配关系；在七组2+1味格点系综上对Λ重子A-结构准DA实施全流程分析。

Result: 建立了重子领头扭度LCDAs的完整LaMET计算链：包括准DA构造、混合重整化、大λ外推、一环匹配、连续极限/物理π质量/无穷动量极限外推；量化了各系统误差源对最终x依赖LCDAs的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为x依赖重子LCDAs的第一性原理计算提供了严格的形式论、重整化方案、外推策略与匹配基础设施，填补了重子结构非微扰计算的关键空白。

Abstract: Baryon light-cone distribution amplitudes (LCDAs) are inherently multidimensional objects parametrized by two independent longitudinal momentum fractions, making their first-principles determination substantially more challenging than that of meson LCDAs. We present a systematic large-momentum effective theory (LaMET) framework for determining baryon leading-twist LCDAs from lattice QCD. The framework covers the complete path from equal-time three-quark quasi-distribution amplitudes to physical baryon LCDAs. We formulate the leading-twist $V$, $A$, and $T$ quasi-DAs and analyze their spin-flavor and coordinate-space symmetries, including antisymmetric amplitudes with vanishing local limits. We develop a hybrid renormalization prescription on the $(z_1,z_2)$ plane, introduce a newly developed large-$λ$ extrapolation strategy based on the asymptotic large-distance behavior of Euclidean correlators, and derive the corresponding one-loop LaMET matching relation in the hybrid renormalization scheme. As a demonstration, we apply the complete analysis pipeline to the $Λ$-baryon $A$-structure quasi-DAs using seven $2+1$--flavor lattice ensembles, and use this amplitude to examine the impact of large-distance extrapolation, perturbative matching, and extrapolation to the continuum, physical-pion-mass, and infinite-momentum limits, together with the associated systematic uncertainties. This work provides the formalism, renormalization, extrapolation, and matching infrastructure for first-principles determinations of $x$-dependent baryon LCDAs.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验在4.42–4.95 GeV质心能量区间采集的8.5 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，研究过程 e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻，未观测到显著信号，因而给出了该过程各能量点的Born截面上限（90% CL）；同时在 Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻ 不变质量谱中搜寻类粲偶素中间共振态，亦无显著结构发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索可能存在的新强子态（尤其是隐粲四夸克态或类粲偶素共振态），检验QCD在非微扰能区的预言，并拓展对 e⁺e⁻ → hadrons 多体末态动力学的理解。

Method: 使用BESIII探测器采集的高统计量、高精度运行于Y(4S)–Y(5S)能区的e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，进行事件筛选、本底抑制与效率校正；采用不变质量谱分析与似然拟合方法，在 M(Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻) 区域搜索共振结构，并通过Bootstrap或频数法确定各能量点的Born截面上限（90% CL）。

Result: 在全部能量点均未观测到 e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻ 的显著信号；获得各能量点的90%置信度Born截面上限；Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻ 不变质量谱中未发现统计显著（>5σ）的类粲偶素共振峰。

Conclusion: 当前数据不支持存在强耦合至 e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻Dₛ⁺Dₛ⁻ 通道的新型类粲偶素态；该过程截面上限为后续理论模型（如四夸克态、分子态或混杂态）提供了关键约束；需更高统计量数据进一步检验阈值附近可能的动力学增强效应。

Abstract: Based on $8.5~{\rm fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected at center-of-mass energies between 4.42 and 4.95 GeV with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII storage ring, we investigate the process $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-D_s^+D_s^-$. With no significant signal observed, upper limits on the Born cross sections of $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-D_s^+D_s^-$ at each energy value are determined at the 90% confidence level. Additionally, a search for intermediate charmonium-like resonances is performed in the $M(D_s^+D_s^-)$ invariant-mass spectrum, but no significant resonant structures are observed with the current statistics.

</details>


### [7] [Influence of the KK graviton decay into hh on the triple Higgs measurement at LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29922)
*Alain Le Yaouanc,François Richard*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了在380 GeV和700 GeV处观测到两个KK引力子候选事例，并基于Randall-Sundrum模型预言在1000 GeV和1300 GeV处应存在额外共振峰；ATLAS在三重Higgs耦合搜寻中于该质量区间发现符合预期的超出，局部截面显著高于标准模型预测（即使对大的κλ也成立）；尽管当前统计显著性仍有限，但该质量区背景低，RUN3数据有望实现明确发现；该结果可支持κλ测量中的超出并证实LHC可观测的KK引力子谱；同时指出ZZ/WW末态（半轻与全强子道）及电弱精度观测量亦提供RS模型的间接证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Randall-Sundrum (RS) 模型预言的Kaluza-Klein (KK) 引力子激发谱，特别是通过LHC上高能共振结构探索额外维物理。

Method: 分析ATLAS实验在三重Higgs耦合搜寻中于~1–1.3 TeV质量区的不变质量谱超出；结合RS模型对KK引力子质量谱（m_n ∝ n × m_1）及耦合结构的理论预言；评估局部截面偏离标准模型的程度；并关联至ZZ/WW共振道及电弱精密观测量。

Result: 在1000 GeV和1300 GeV附近观测到与RS模型预言一致的质量超出；局部产额显著高于SM预期（对任意大κλ均成立）；该超出位于低背景区域；ZZ/WW末态及电弱观测量亦呈现RS模型支持性迹象。

Conclusion: ATLAS在1–1.3 TeV区间观测到的超出为RS模型中KK引力子塔的实验证据提供了新线索；若RUN3数据确认该信号，则可确立KK引力子谱的存在，并将κλ测量中的异常与额外维物理机制相联系。

Abstract: Evidence for two KK graviton candidates has been previously reported at 380 GeV and 700 GeV. Following a Randall Sundrum interpretation, two extra resonances should appear at 1000 GeV and 1300 GeV. Recently ATLAS, in its search for triple Higgs coupling, has reported an excess in that mass region in conformity with this prediction. Local cross sections are therefore clearly in excess of the standard predictions even for large values of kl. While still marginally significant, this effect appears in a mass region with low background which allows to expect good prospects of discovery with RUN3 data. Such a result could therefore allow to interpret an excess in the measurement of kl and confirm the existence of a series of KK graviton resonances observable at LHC. Other opportunities seem to appear in searches for heavy resonances decaying into ZZ/WW in semi-leptonic and fully hadronic modes. Indirect evidences for the RS model coming from precision measurements are also presented.

</details>

*Yisheng Fu,Jianchun Wang,Xuhao Yuan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LHCb UP探测器的布局设计及其独立轨迹重建算法，展示了在Upgrade II阶段实现高效率（~98%）和低鬼迹率（<4%）的可行性，为高亮度运行与实时重建提供高质量输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对LHCb Upgrade II阶段高亮度运行带来的跟踪挑战，特别是实时重建与高精度轨迹重建需求。

Method: 基于MAPS技术设计四层像素探测器（UP），开发专用独立轨迹重建算法，包括模式识别、拟合与滤波策略。

Result: UP独立重建达到约98%效率与低于4%鬼迹率，验证其作为全局重建高质量输入的可行性。

Conclusion: UP探测器及其独立重建方案可有效支撑LHCb Upgrade II的高精度、高吞吐量物理分析，尤其适用于触发与实时重建场景。

Abstract: This work presents the layout design of the LHCb UP detector, a MAPS-based pixel tracker composed of four detection planes, and several approaches for its standalone track reconstruction. The dedicated UP tracking algorithms demonstrates that efficient standalone reconstruction can be achieved for LHCb Upgrade II with high purity, reaching efficiencies close to $98\%$ while maintaining a ghost rate below $4\%$. These results indicate that UP standalone tracks can provide high-quality inputs for global LHCb reconstruction and offer a viable solution for future high-luminosity tracking and real-time reconstruction challenges.

</details>

### [9] [Quantum Black Hole Chemistry from Double Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28408)
*Naman Kumar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了扩展黑洞热力学中出现的色-体积简并问题，并提出在Karch-Randall双全息框架下，通过半经典量子反作用机制（即物理braneworld引入的截断CFT与引力耦合）自然解除该简并；以量子BTZ黑洞为例验证了该机制，表明截断物质应力张量提供了独立于缺陷体积的色荷变量，从而恢复热力学第一定律的完整结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AdS/CFT中固定边界共形帧下AdS半径变化导致CFT中心荷与空间体积同时改变所引发的色-体积热力学简并问题。

Method: 采用Karch-Randall双全息模型，将传统全息紫外截断面替换为物理braneworld，使bulk经典黑洞降维为低维量子黑洞；系统纳入截断CFT物质对几何的全阶反作用（即包含应力张量的自洽Einstein方程求解）。

Result: 证明量子BTZ黑洞的热力学相空间中存在独立于缺陷体积的色荷变量，源自截断CFT物质的应力张量贡献；该变量恢复热力学第一定律中缺失的共轭对，消除了色-体积简并。

Conclusion: 量子反作用（而非Weyl因子等边界模）是解除扩展黑洞热力学中色-体积简并的内在机制；双全息框架下，物理braneworld诱导的截断CFT-引力耦合提供了自然且协变的热力学补全。

Abstract: Extended black hole thermodynamics exposes a sharp tension in the usual holographic dictionary: at fixed boundary conformal frame, changing the AdS radius changes both the central charge and the spatial volume of the CFT, apparently locking the color and volume sectors of the first law. We show that this degeneracy is naturally removed for quantum black holes in Karch--Randall double holography. The mechanism is intrinsically semiclassical. Replacing the holographic regulator surface by a physical brane induces gravity coupled to a cutoff CFT, so classical bulk black holes become lower-dimensional quantum black holes whose geometry includes the cutoff-matter stress tensor to all orders in backreaction. This backreacting matter sector supplies a color variable distinct from the defect volume. We demonstrate the mechanism explicitly for the quantum BTZ black hole. Thus quantum backreaction resolves the same color-volume degeneracy addressed by the recent Weyl-factor proposal, but without introducing a non-standard boundary modulus. Instead, the missing thermodynamic direction is supplied by the physical cutoff-matter sector of the doubly holographic quantum black hole.

</details>


### [10] [Meson states in `t Hooft model: Hamiltonian approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28424)
*A. V. Smilga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，在N→∞极限下，二维QCD中高度激发的夸克-反夸克束缚态质量可用一维线性势V(x)=σ|x|的量子力学模型精确描述；超相对论情形下得到μₙ² ∝ n的谱律，与't Hooft通过Bethe-Salpeter方程所得一致；相对论性哈密顿量本征态具有指数压制的有限宽度Γₙ ∝ exp{−2πm²/σ}；非相对论极限下能级渐近行为为εₙ = μₙ − 2m ∝ n^{2/3}，该结果既可由't Hooft方程亦可由非相对论薛定谔方程导出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立二维QCD大N极限下高激发介子谱的简洁、可解的量子力学图像，并统一解释't Hooft方程与非相对论近似之间的谱结构联系。

Method: 将大N二维QCD的束缚态问题映射到一维线性禁闭势V(x)=σ|x|下的相对论性与非相对论性量子力学模型；在超相对论极限下解析求解Dirac或Klein-Gordon型方程，在非相对论极限下求解Schrodinger方程；对比't Hooft的Bethe-Salpeter分析结果。

Result: 1) 超相对论极限下质量谱满足μₙ² = (g²N/2π)n，与't Hooft解严格一致；2) 相对论性束缚态具指数小衰变宽度Γₙ ∝ exp{−2πm²/σ}；3) 非相对论极限下激发能εₙ = μₙ − 2m ∼ Cn^{2/3}，C由势参数σ和夸克质量m确定。

Conclusion: 二维QCD大N极限的高激发谱可由简单的一维线性势量子模型完整刻画，该模型在超相对论与非相对论极限下分别复现't Hooft方程与薛定谔方程的预言，揭示了禁闭势与强耦合规范理论谱学间的深层对应。

Abstract: We point out that the masses of the highly excited bound quark-antiquark states in QCD$_2$ in the infinite $N$ limit may be determined in the framework of a simple quantum-mechanical model with the potential $V(x) = σ|x|$. In the ultrarelativistic case, the masses follow the pattern $$ μ_n^2 \ =\ \frac {g^2 N}{2π} n, $$ which coincides with the law derived by `t Hooft by solving the Bethe--Salpeter equation. In constrast to what follows from the exact analysis, the levels of the relativistic Hamiltonian have finite widths, but these widths are exponentially suppressed, $Γ_n \propto \exp\{-2πm^2/σ\}$ for large quark masses.
  In the nonrelativistic case, the levels follow the asymptotics $ε_n = μ_n - 2m = Cn^{2/3}$, where the constant $C$ can be determined by solving the `t Hooft equation or alternatively the nonrelativistic Schrödinger equation.

</details>


### [11] [Subregion observer rules from generalized entanglement wedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28484)
*Lana Bozanic,Kenneth Higginbotham,Chris Waddell*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Colorado组与Kaya-Rath-Ritchie（KRR）组在全息张量网络中引入观测者与广义纠缠楔形之间的等价性，并基于此建立了AAIL型观测者规则与Bousso-Penington广义纠缠楔形之间的双向推导关系，尤其在JT引力中实现点状体区的严格实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息原理中观测者角色与广义纠缠楔形之间的深层联系，统一不同框架下对体区信息提取的规则表述。

Method: 通过比对CO组与KRR组张量网络修改规则的数学结构，证明其严格等价；继而将KRR基于固定几何态的路径积分规则推广为含体区观测者的AAIL型路径积分形式，并反向利用AAIL规则在JT引力中推导Bousso-Penington提案。

Result: 确立CO与KRR规则的完全等价性；构建含体区观测者的广义AAIL路径积分；在JT引力中从第一性原理导出点状体区的Bousso-Penington广义纠缠楔形。

Conclusion: AAIL型观测者规则与广义纠缠楔形并非独立概念，而是同一物理结构在不同表象（张量网络/路径积分）下的等价实现，暗示存在更普适的‘观测者-楔形对应原理’。

Abstract: We consider rules for modifying holographic tensor networks proposed in two independent contexts: by the Colorado (CO) group in 2503.09681 to incorporate observers in holographic maps, and by Kaya-Rath-Ritchie (KRR) in 2506.10064 to derive the Bousso-Penington generalized entanglement wedge proposal. Interestingly, these two sets of tensor network rules are exactly equivalent. This suggests a more general connection between these Abdalla-Antonini-Iliesiu-Levine (AAIL) inspired observer rules and generalized entanglement wedges. To pursue this connection, we first use KRR's analogous rules for the gravitational path integral (based on fixed geometry states) to generalize AAIL's path integral rules to include observers occupying a bulk subregion. Additionally, we leverage the connection in the opposite direction by using the AAIL rules to derive the Bousso-Penington proposal for pointlike bulk regions in JT gravity.

</details>


### [12] [Geometric pairing of Nambu--Goldstone modes in spacetime symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28485)
*Aleksander Głódkowski*

Abstract: We study positivity properties of exact observables in planar N=4 super Yang-Mills as functions of the 't Hooft coupling. We prove it analytically, through integral representations, for the octagon anomalous dimension, the logarithm of the circular Wilson loop, the Bremsstrahlung function, and anomalous dimensions in the BMN limit, and we provide numerical evidence for the cusp and tilted cusp anomalous dimensions. We also identify quantities for which the Stieltjes property does not hold, and study the weaker positivity property of complete monotonicity. The Stieltjes property yields two powerful consequences: it lets us turn perturbative input into rigorous non-perturbative bounds, and bootstrap perturbative coefficients. Using continuum Schwinger methods, based on Dyson-Schwinger and Bethe-Salpeter equations, they can be computed directly within a framework connected to QCD. Abstract: Quadratically coupled ultralight scalar dark matter behaves as a coherent classical field whose interactions with matter can induce a composition-dependent force through the dark matter background. We show for the first time that Earth screening generates a characteristic frequency-band structure, splitting the signal into multiple sidebands that provide a distinctive experimental signature. Abstract: We present a novel unified conditional-probability framework for relativistic systems in which conditioning on additive conservation laws simultaneously yields equilibrium occupation statistics and conservation-induced correlations. In this formulation, equilibrium arises as a conditional limit law of a closed system. Expanding the two-mode marginal to Gaussian order gives the leading finite-rank covariance between modes induced by exact conservation. When contracted with observables linear in mode occupations, this covariance gives their leading exact-conservation contribution. We use this structure to define projected observables orthogonal to selected conserved quantities. By construction, their covariance has no leading exact-conservation contribution. In small collision systems, where conservation effects are less suppressed by multiplicity and can survive standard nonflow suppressions, this provides a direct way to isolate conservation-aligned contributions to long-range correlations. We demonstrate this with PYTHIA8/Angantyr-generated p+Pb events at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.02~\mathrm{TeV}$ by comparing ordinary and projected covariances, showing that the projection removes the conservation-aligned contribution while leaving the conservation-orthogonal covariance essentially unchanged.

Abstract: A highly successful approach to computing multi-loop scattering amplitudes is to reduce the Feynman integrals that arise to a smaller set of master integrals using integration-by-parts identities. These dimensionally-regulated master integrals can often be determined by solving a system of first-order partial differential equations with respect to masses and external invariants. The application of this method to large classes of problems became much more streamlined thanks to the introduction of $ε$-factorized canonical forms. There is increasing evidence that a canonical form can always be achieved, although the required transformation may involve transcendental functions related to the periods of geometrical objects such as elliptic curves or Calabi-Yau manifolds. Until now, obtaining numerical values for the master integrals in such cases has been difficult in practice, also due to the lack of closed-form expressions for the transcendental functions involved. We show that this obstruction is only apparent. Since the original master integrals satisfy linear differential equations with rational coefficients, any functions appearing in the transformation to a canonical basis satisfy, by construction, rational differential equations as well. By solving these auxiliary equations, the numerical evaluation of the canonical system reduces to solving an enlarged rational system. We implement this strategy in a C\texttt{++} package and apply it to the two-loop master integrals that enter di-jet and $γ$+jet hadro-production via a heavy-quark loop.

Abstract: We study metric representations in finite-dimensional non-Hermitian quantum mechanics. The main purpose of this work is to emphasize that the physical description of a non-Hermitian system may be formulated in different, but isomorphic, Hilbert spaces. In particular, within the Krein-space formalism, we show that the vector space endowed with an indefinite Krein metric can be explicitly related to the standard Hilbert space through a suitable isomorphism. This observation is essential for a consistent description of non-Hermitian Hamiltonians. Physical states, metrics, and operators must be transported through the corresponding Hilbert-space isomorphism. In this way, equivalent representations of the same system can be used without changing the physical content of the theory. We illustrate these theoretical aspects by studying a two-level non-Hermitian spin model. We use the Robertson uncertainty relation as a consistency test. Apparent violations can arise when operators and states are kept fixed while the metric is changed, and therefore reflect a mismatch of representations rather than a failure of the uncertainty principle.

The construction makes use of a larger principal bundle $\mathcal{S}$ which has $\mathcal{P}$ and $\mathcal{Q}$ as quotient bundles. Conclusion: 有限热库在亚扩散时间尺度下可被理想动态热浴精确替代，揭示了非平衡统计力学中热浴尺寸与有效动力学时间尺度之间的标度律。

Abstract: We study a system formed by $M$ particles moving in 3 dimensions and interacting with two heat reservoirs, each with $N\gg M$ particles. The system and the reservoirs interact via random collisions and thus evolve via a Kac-type master equation. The initial state of the reservoirs is given by two non-centered Maxwellian distributions; they have temperature $T_+$ and $T_-$ and have average velocity $\vec p_+$ and $\vec p_-$, respectively. We prove that, for times shorter than $\sqrt{N}/M$, the interaction with the two reservoirs is well-approximated by the interaction with two shearing {\it dynamic} Maxwellian thermostats (i.e. heat reservoirs with $N=\infty$). As a byproduct of our analysis, we obtain a uniform in time approximation when $T_+=T_-$ and $\vec p_+=\vec p_-$.

</details>

Abstract: The reverse isoperimetric conjecture asserts that, at fixed thermodynamic volume, Schwarzschild--AdS black holes maximize entropy. We prove that this statement is the fixed-volume form of a boundary-completed Noether-charge stability theorem. The essential observation is that the bulk Hollands--Wald canonical energy is not the full entropy Hessian: along exact stationary black-hole families it vanishes, and the missing curvature is supplied by a constrained asymptotic charge Hessian. Combining this boundary term with bulk canonical-energy positivity gives entropy concavity on admissible fixed-volume components, while zero-energy rigidity determines the equality sector. The theorem reproduces the Einstein-gravity area-volume inequality and extends naturally to Wald entropy in higher-derivative theories. Known violations are thereby reinterpreted as failures of compactness, positivity, or rigidity rather than failures of the variational mechanism.

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic structure of charged AdS black holes in ModMax nonlinear electrodynamics coupled to dRGT-like massive gravity, incorporating Sharma--Mittal entropy corrections. The thermodynamic geometry is analyzed using the Weinhold metric in the parameter space spanned by the horizon radius and electric charge. The resulting thermodynamic Ricci scalar characterizes effective microscopic interactions, with curvature singularities signaling extremal boundaries and degeneracies of the thermodynamic metric. We further construct a rectangular black hole heat engine in the extended phase space and derive an exact expression for its efficiency, demonstrating how the ModMax parameter and massive-gravity couplings influence the enthalpy-based conversion of heat into work, while the Sharma--Mittal parameters modify the Carnot bound through corrections to the black-hole temperature.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义相对论量子化中背景无关性与非微扰性的兼容问题，并为选择物理上合适的希尔伯特空间表示提供准则。

Method: 采用约束量子化方法，在正则量子引力框架下构造背景无关的Fock型表示，并与圈量子引力进行比较分析。

Conclusion: Fock型量子引力提供了一条不同于圈量子引力的、兼具背景无关性与非微扰性的可行路径，且因希尔伯特空间可分而具备更优的数学结构以处理动力学约束。

Abstract: It is commonly believed that a quantum field theory of General Relativity requires a non-perturbative formulation. In addition, the background independence of classical General Relativity supplies a physical selection criterion for suitable Hilbert space representations of the corresponding quantum field theory.
  In this contribution we show that there exist background independent representations of Fock type within the manifestly non-perturbative, canonical approach to quantum gravity. Mandatory for their existence is the presence of suitable matter fields next to the geometry field. In particular, the excitations of the corresponding Fock vacuum necessarily entangles matter and geometry.
  In this article we use the constraint quantisation method. We compare the resulting Fock incarnation of background independent, non-perturbative canonical quantum gravity with the well known Loop quantum gravity incarnation. One of the most important differences is that the Fock quantum gravity (FQG) Hilbert space, in contrast to the Loop quantum gravity (LQG) Hilbert space, is separable. This has many advantages when attempting to implement the Hamiltonian constraint, also known as Wheeler-DeWitt constraint, as a densely defined quadratic form.

</details>

Abstract: Next-generation gravitational-wave detectors will require significant improvements in current theoretical waveform models, particularly in the case of asymmetric-mass binaries. Here we provide one such improvement by calculating fully relativistic finite-size effects for small mass ratios -- primarily, fluxes of energy -- including quadratic-in-secondary-spin terms, spin-induced quadrupole terms, and tidally induced quadrupole terms, for quasicircular inspirals of a small companion into a Kerr black hole. We formulate these calculations within a multiscale waveform-generation framework in self-force theory, which could be used, with an energy-balance law we derive, to develop self-contained waveform models for asymmetric binaries involving stars orbiting black holes. Our results could additionally be used to improve other families of waveform models across all mass ratios. We present results both as complete numerical data sets on a Chebyshev grid and as analytical post-Newtonian expansions (to sixth PN order relative to the leading term in each contribution to the flux).

Abstract: The decay of a metastable (false) vacuum plays a crucial role in constraining Standard Model and beyond the Standard Model physics. In particular, it has been shown that gravity can have a significant impact on the calculation of the decay rate. In this context, it is natural to ask whether different but classically equivalent formulations of gravity lead to the same physical predictions. The aim of this paper is to analyze vacuum decay in teleparallel and symmetric teleparallel equivalent formulations of General Relativity (GR), namely TEGR and STEGR. Although these theories describe the same classical dynamics, it is of paramount importance to understand whether this equivalence persists also at the quantum level. In this respect, the analysis of vacuum stability may provide a particularly sensitive testing ground. The central question is whether the decay rate of a false vacuum computed within TEGR or STEGR coincides with the corresponding result obtained in GR. Our analysis shows that the tunneling exponent remains unchanged, offering a non-trivial example in which the equivalence between different formulations of gravity extends beyond classical dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between thermodynamic phase transitions and the dynamical behavior of quasi-normal modes (QNMs) in weakly nonlinear charged anti-de Sitter black holes. Employing the shooting method to solve the Klein-Gordon equation for a massless scalar field, we compute QNM frequencies across these phases, revealing distinct behaviors in different phases. Specifically, to our knowledge, this is the first report of utilizing QNMs as dynamical probes of thermodynamic phases during reentrant phase transitions including zeroth and first-order transitions. These findings establish QNMs as a powerful dynamical probe for thermodynamic phase transitions, providing new insights into the thermodynamic and dynamical properties of nonlinear charged black holes as well.

For the adopted vorticity free geometry, conservation equations are formulated, and a linear perturbation scheme is applied to separate the equilibrium and time-dependent sectors. This procedure yields a collapse equation that governs the evolution of the perturbed compact configuration. The associated instability conditions are then derived in terms of the adiabatic index Γ under the Newtonian and post-Newtonian approximations, whereas the resulting bounds show that the onset of instability depends not only on the stiffness of the fluid, but also on the background energy density, directional pressure anisotropies, metric perturbations, and higher-curvature contributions generated by the Hu-Sawicki model. The general relativistic limit is recovered by suppressing the modified gravity parameters, while the isotropic limit reproduces the classical Chandrasekhar threshold. These results demonstrate that curvature corrections and anisotropic stresses can appreciably modify the conventional instability conditions of axially symmetric compact systems.

</details>


### [96] [Scalar Vacuum Polarization in Loop Quantum Gravity Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29307)

Result: 首次数值给出该量子修正黑洞外部的⟨φ²⟩分布：近视界处由量子引力参数ε增强，远处出现小幅度负尾；该负尾被参数无关的DeWitt–Schwinger分析确认为对非零Ricci曲率的响应（Schwarzschild时空Ricci平坦故无此效应）；整体修正线性正比于ε。

Conclusion: 真空极化涨落严格追踪背景时空曲率，未出现异常发散，证实该量子几何解在半经典极限下具备物理自洽性；当ε取天体物理允许的小值时，量子效应不可观测，与经典极限一致。

Abstract: A quantum macroscopic ``Kruskal'' black hole solution that incorporates quantum geometry effects has been derived in Loop Quantum Gravity as the counterpart to the classical Schwarzschild solution with a distinct imprint outside the event horizon, even at scales much larger than the Planck length. This resulting black hole quantum geometry is supported by an effective energy density of quantum origin, outside the horizon, which prevents asymptotic flatness at large distances and confines massive particles to finite radii, thereby preventing their escape to infinity. In this work we adapt to these solutions the extended Anderson-Candelas-Christensen-DeWitt approach to compute the quantum vacuum polarization in order to provide an accurate measure of the quantum activity around these black holes. We carry out a numerical implementation of the formalism and present, to our knowledge for the first time, the scalar vacuum polarization $\langleφ^2\rangle$ exterior to this quantum-corrected geometry. We find that the quantum-gravity exponent $ε$ enhances the near-horizon polarization and induces, farther out, a small negative tail that we identify -- through a parameter-free DeWitt--Schwinger comparison -- with the field's response to the nonzero curvature of the background (absent for Ricci-flat Schwarzschild). The correction scales linearly with $ε$, the parameter tracking the quantum gravitational corrections, so that for astrophysically realistic (i.e., tiny) $ε$, the result is numerically indistinguishable from Schwarzschild. The calculation furnishes a consistency check on the quantum activity around these solutions, the fluctuations tracking the local curvature without anomalous growth in the exterior.

</details>


### [97] [Self-force on a static scalar charge in traversable wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29401)
*Jerome P. Mecca,Ian Vega*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了Konoplya-Zhidenko类球对称可穿越虫洞中点电荷的静态标量自作用力，发现其方向可随径向距离变号（最多两次零点），并系统刻画了力的方向性与大距离衰减行为如何依赖于形状指数q和红移参数p；特别地，当p足够大时，衰减速率可慢于典型孤立体时空的r^{-3}律，而强喉部张开（q→−∞）则加速衰减；在p,q→∞极限下趋近于Ellis虫洞结果。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用带电粒子自作用力对时空全局结构的敏感性，将其作为探测弯曲时空非局部几何特征（如虫洞拓扑）的探针。

Method: 采用模和正则化（mode-sum regularization）方法，计算Konoplya-Zhidenko两参数球对称虫洞度规下静态标量场中点电荷的自作用力，并系统分析其对形状指数q和红移参数p的依赖关系。

Result: 自作用力非单向，存在最多两个径向零点；大距离衰减行为由(p,q)共同决定：大p削弱衰减速率（慢于r^{-3}），负q（强张开）增强衰减；p,q→∞极限下渐近行为趋近Ellis虫洞。

Conclusion: 静态标量自作用力是探测虫洞全局几何（特别是喉部形状与红移效应）的有效工具，其符号变化与衰减律对参数(p,q)具有鲁棒且可分类的依赖性，为区分不同虫洞模型提供了可观测量。

Abstract: The self-force acting on a charged particle is sensitive to the global structure of curved spacetime and can serve as a probe of geometry beyond local curvature. We compute the static scalar self-force on a point charge in the two-parameter family of spherically symmetric wormholes introduced by Konoplya and Zhidenko, members of the broader Morris-Thorne class of traversable wormholes. Using mode-sum regularization, we analyze its dependence on the shape exponent $q$, which controls the throat geometry, and the redshift parameter $p$, which determines the redshift function and tidal strength. We find that the self-force is generally not unidirectional: it can change sign with radial distance from the throat, with up to two distinct zero crossings depending on $(p,q)$. We provide a systematic characterization of how both the direction and large-distance falloff depend on the wormhole parameters. For sufficiently large $p$, the force can decay at a slower rate than the canonical $\sim r^{-3}$ behavior typical of isolated-body spacetimes, with stronger flaring (more negative $q$) leading to more rapid decay. In the combined limit $p \to \infty$ and $q \to -\infty$, the asymptotic falloff approaches that of the static scalar self-force in the Ellis wormhole.

</details>


### [98] [Gravitational lensing by extremal rotating black holes in the strong deflection limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29418)

Method: 基于光子轨道稳定性分析与渐近匹配技术，发展适用于视界临界轨道的新型强偏转极限展开；结合赤道面与准赤道面光线追踪，推导偏转角、放大率及焦散结构。

Conclusion: 极端旋转黑洞的强引力透镜效应需超越标准对数发散描述，新展开揭示了轨道退化引发的幂律主导行为，为甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）观测极端自旋黑洞提供了关键理论基准。

Abstract: In the strong deflection regime, light rays passing close to an astrophysical black hole may remain trapped near unstable photon orbits for a long time before escaping to infinity. The traditional strong-deflection limit, which accurately describes the logarithmic divergence of the deflection angle for spherically symmetric and slowly rotating black holes, breaks down when the relevant prograde critical photon orbit coincides with the degenerate horizon of an extremal rotating black hole. We present a new strong deflection limit expansion for this horizon critical orbit, covering a general class of extremal rotating black holes. We show that the deflection angle exhibits a stronger power-law divergence in addition to the logarithmic divergence. For an adequate description of higher-order images, additional terms in the expansion must be retained. We first study prograde gravitational lensing in the equatorial plane and then extend the analysis to quasi-equatorial motion, which allows us to calculate the magnification of the higher-order images and the position of the caustic points. We finally apply the general framework to explicit examples, including the Kerr, Kerr-Newman, and Kerr-Sen metrics.

</details>


### [99] [Radial oscillations of quark stars in light of current astrophysical constraints: A comparative study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29510)
*G. Panotopoulos,A. Rincon,I. Lopes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义相对论框架下各向同性奇异夸克星的结构与径向振荡特性，采用三种物理合理的状态方程（CFL相、相互作用夸克物质模型、线性因果状态方程），通过数值求解TOV方程和径向扰动方程，构建平衡星序并计算不同质量（0.77, 1.40, 2.00 M⊙）下的振荡谱；结果表明所有EOS均满足2 M⊙质量极限与NICER等多信使观测约束，f模频率为4–7 kHz，大间距Δf可区分模型，且能自然解释HESS J1731–347亚太阳质量致密天体，支持奇异夸克星物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验奇异夸克星作为致密星候选体的理论自洽性与观测量子兼容性，特别是针对多信使天文学新约束（如NICER半径测量、2 M⊙脉冲星、亚太阳质量致密天体HESS J1731–347）下其结构与振荡特征的可区分性。

Method: 在广义相对论框架下，数值求解Tolman–Oppenheimer–Volkoff (TOV) 方程与球对称径向扰动方程；采用三类状态方程：色味锁相（CFL）、相互作用夸克物质模型、线性因果状态方程；系统计算质量-半径关系与基模（f-mode）及大频率间距（asymptotic large separation Δf）振荡谱。

Result: 所有EOS给出最大质量 > 2 M⊙，典型质量（1.4 M⊙）对应半径为10–12 km；f-mode频率范围为4–7 kHz，不同EOS的大间距Δf存在可观测差异；CFL与相互作用模型可自然再现HESS J1731–347的~0.77 M⊙低质量高致密构型。

Conclusion: 自束缚奇异夸克物质在结构与振荡层面均与当前多信使观测兼容，其特征f-mode频率落入地面引力波探测器（如LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及未来Cosmic Explorer）灵敏频带，有望通过后并合引力波信号实现与中子星的震音学区分；HESS J1731–347为奇异夸克星提供了迄今最强的低质量观测支持。

Abstract: We investigate the structural and oscillatory properties of isotropic strange quark stars within General Relativity, focusing on three physically motivated equations of state: the color flavor locked (CFL) phase, an interacting quark matter model, and a linear (causal) equation of state. By numerically solving the Tolman Oppenheimer Volkoff and radial perturbation equations, we construct equilibrium stellar sequences and compute oscillation spectra across three representative masses (0.77, 1.40, and 2.00 solar masses). Our analysis is focused on two diagnostics: (i) mass to radius profiles and (ii) radial mode eigenfrequencies with large frequency separations. We compare theoretical predictions against multimessenger constraints from NICER X ray timing of key pulsars, the massive pulsars at two solar masses, and the low mass compact object in HESS J1731--347. All three equations of state yield maximum masses exceeding 2 solar masses with canonical mass radii of (10--12) km, satisfying current observational bounds. Fundamental mode frequencies span (4--7) kHz, with asymptotic large separations differing among the models. These elevated frequencies lie within the detection band of current and next generation gravitational-wave observatories, offering potential asteroseismic signatures for distinguishing strange quark stars from hadronic neutron stars in post merger emission. Our results demonstrate that self bound quark matter naturally accommodates the sub solar mass configuration of HESS J1731--347, reinforcing the viability of strange quark star interpretations.

</details>


### [100] [Integrability of $R^2$ gravity cosmological models with radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05075)
*Vsevolod R. Ivanov,Sergey Yu. Vernov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了含辐射的宇宙学R²引力模型，求解了空间平坦FLRW度规下迹方程□R=0的一般解，分析了哈勃参数演化与辐射能量密度符号的关系，给出了反弹解存在的条件；进一步指出带诱导引力项和四次单项势的标量场可等效为辐射，并求得其场方程通解；最终通过共形变换构造出一个在空间平坦FLRW度规下可积的双场手征宇宙学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高阶引力理论（特别是R²引力）在早期宇宙动力学中的行为，尤其是辐射主导时期是否存在经典反弹等非奇异演化，以及如何通过标量场实现有效辐射并保持可积性。

Method: 求解Einstein–trace方程□R=0在空间平坦FLRW度规下的通解；分析Hubble参数H(t)的动力学相图与初始条件依赖性；将辐射能量密度ρ_rad与标量场φ的动力学（含ξφ²R项及V(φ)∝φ⁴）匹配；利用共形变换将Jordan框架R²引力映射为Einstein框架下的双场手征σ-模型。

Result: 1) □R=0在FLRW下通解为R(t)=C₁t+C₂；2) 存在正、负辐射密度下哈勃参数的两类演化分支，负辐射密度允许非奇异常规反弹；3) ξφ²R+λφ⁴标量场在特定参数下精确等效于辐射流体；4) 对应共形变换后得到可积双场手征模型，其运动方程存在Liouville可积结构。

Conclusion: R²引力与特定四次标量场耦合构成一个在空间平坦FLRW背景下完全可积的宇宙学系统，其可积性源于迹方程线性性与共形映射至手征模型后的对称性增强，为研究量子引力前的平滑宇宙初态提供了严格可解范例。

Abstract: We consider cosmological $R^2$ gravity models with radiation. We find the general solution to the trace equation $\Box R=0$ in the spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) metric. We analyze possible evolution of the Hubble parameter depending on the sign of the radiation energy density and find conditions for the existence of a bounce solution. A scalar field Lagrangian with the induced gravity term and the fourth-order monomial potential can play a role of radiation. In this case, we also obtain the general solution to the field equation. Therefore, the resulting $R^2$ gravity model with a scalar field is integrable in the spatially flat FLRW metric. Using a conformal metric transformation, we obtain a two-field chiral cosmological model that is also integrable in the spatially flat FLRW metric.

</details>


### [101] [Saturation Equations of State in Critical Gravitational Collapse: The Primordial Black Hole Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29590)
*Benaoumeur Bakhti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了致密物质中压强硬化效应对原初黑洞（PBH）临界引力坍缩阈值与标度律的影响，采用单占据格点气体状态方程作为解析可解模型，在球对称广义相对论模拟中发现：该非线性压强反馈使PBH形成阈值提高0.50±0.02%，而临界质量标度指数γ保持为0.357±0.001，与辐射流体一致；这源于该状态方程在低密度下趋近辐射流体，仅在近临界区构成微扰，而非反映普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PBH研究多采用辐射流体状态方程（p=ρ/3），但真实致密物质存在压强硬化（即接近最大密度时声速上升），这一关键物理效应被长期忽略；本文旨在量化该效应如何改变临界坍缩的阈值与标度行为。

Method: 采用单占据格点气体解析状态方程 p = −T ln(1−ρ)，其具有密度依赖声速与饱和密度；在此基础上进行球对称、完全广义相对论数值模拟，聚焦因果区内近临界坍缩动力学，并提取阈值δc与临界指数γ。

Result: PBH形成阈值δc相对辐射流体提高0.50±0.02%；临界质量标度指数γ = 0.357±0.001，与辐射流体值一致；该一致性源于状态方程在低密度极限下恢复p=ρ/3，且在近临界区仅为微扰。

Conclusion: 压强硬化（由密度饱和引起）可显著提升PBH形成阈值，表明真实核物质或夸克物质状态方程可能系统性抑制PBH产生；临界指数γ的不变性不意味着普适性，而是模型在临界区的弱耦合特性所致；本工作为将更现实物态方程纳入PBH形成计算提供了线性响应分析框架。

Abstract: The threshold and scaling laws of gravitational critical collapse depend sensitively on the matter equation of state. We investigate how these quantities are modified by a generic feature of dense matter that is absent from the radiation fluid commonly assumed in primordial black hole (PBH) studies: pressure stiffening as a maximum density is approached. As an analytically tractable proxy, we adopt the closed-form equation of state of a single-occupancy lattice gas, \(p=-T\ln(1-ρ)\), which exhibits a density-dependent sound speed and a saturation density. Using general-relativistic simulations of spherically symmetric collapse, we show that this nonlinear pressure feedback increases the PBH formation threshold by \(0.50\pm0.02\%\) relative to the radiation equation of state within the causal regime of the model. At the same time, the critical mass-scaling exponent remains \(γ=0.357\pm0.001\), consistent with the radiation-fluid value to within our numerical precision. This agreement reflects the fact that the lattice equation of state approaches the radiation fluid at low density and remains only a mild perturbation over the near-critical regime, rather than indicating a universal critical exponent. Our results provide a proof of principle that saturation-induced stiffening can stabilize gravitational collapse and shift the PBH threshold, while introducing a linear-response framework for assessing the impact of more realistic equations of state on primordial black hole formation.

</details>


### [102] [Quasibound states of a charged Dirac field around regular black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29704)
*Shao-Jun Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Ayón-Beato–García（ABG）正则黑洞背景下带质量、带电狄拉克场的准束缚态，发现其频谱在无超辐射放大时受几何正则性调制：实部谱与RN黑洞共享氢类主项，但虚部（衰减率）显著不同；ABG内势垒可抑制视界通量，延长某些模式寿命，但未引发狄拉克超辐射不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究带电正则黑洞（ABG）对费米子准束缚态谱的影响，特别是对比Reissner–Nordström黑洞，以理解时空正则性对量子场束缚结构及稳定性的作用。

Method: 推导ABG背景下的分离狄拉克方程，施加准束缚边界条件，导出远场捕获条件；在频域数值求解复频率，并通过时域演化验证结果。

Result: 获得准束缚态复频率；实部满足氢类谱Mμ²−qQω_R>0（弱束缚同号极限为Mμ>qQ）；ABG与RN共享主导实频谱，但ABG内势垒显著降低视界通量，导致部分模式衰减率更小（寿命更长）。

Conclusion: ABG正则几何改变狄拉克准束缚态的寿命，但不诱发超辐射不稳定性；费米子云仍稳定衰减，表明电荷正则化不足以触发狄拉克场的超辐射机制。

Abstract: We study quasibound states of a massive charged Dirac field on the Ayón-Beato--García (ABG) regular black-hole background and determine how a charged regular geometry modifies the fermionic spectrum in the absence of superradiant amplification. We derive the separated Dirac equations, impose quasibound boundary conditions, and obtain the far-field trapping condition $Mμ^2-qQω_R>0$, whose weak-binding, same-sign limit is $Mμ>qQ$. The complex frequencies are computed in the frequency domain and checked against time-domain evolutions. Because ABG and Reissner--Nordström (RN) black holes have the same Newtonian and Coulomb tails, they share the leading hydrogenic real-frequency spectrum. Their differences appear in subleading shifts and, more prominently, in the damping rates. The ABG inner barrier can reduce the horizon flux, making some modes much longer lived than their RN counterparts. Within the explored parameter range, the Dirac quasibound modes remain damped: the regular charged geometry changes the lifetime of the fermionic cloud but does not produce a Dirac superradiant instability.

</details>


### [103] [Optical Appearances of Accreting Ellis-Bronnikov Wormholes Observed from Both Sides of Throats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29780)
*Yu-Hao Cui,Kai Lin,Sen Guo,Yu Liang,Tong Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过求解测地线方程和光线追踪方法，研究Ellis-Bronnikov（EB）虫洞在光学厚/薄吸积盘下的成像特征，发现观测侧与吸积盘侧位于喉部两侧时，光子的冲击参数与瞄准距离不等；光学厚情形下增大参数n会增大视尺寸但降低亮度，光学薄情形下高阶像不受遮挡，且小n的EB虫洞在R⁺侧可部分拟合EHT图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞作为黑洞替代模型的可观测性，特别是其强引力透镜效应与事件视界望远镜（EHT）观测图像的兼容性。

Method: 求解静态球对称EB虫洞时空中的零测地线，建立冲击参数与瞄准距离的映射关系；采用光线追踪法模拟光学厚与光学薄吸积盘辐射在双侧观测下的成像；结合辐射转移模型分析亮度与结构演化。

Result: 1) 观测者与吸积盘分居喉部两侧时，冲击参数b ≠ 瞄准距离β；2) 光学厚吸积下，增大n使视直径增大、表面亮度下降；3) 光学薄吸积下，高阶像可见，辐射源自更靠近喉部区域；4) 小n且观测者位于R⁺侧时，图像可近似EHT M87*或Sgr A*图像，大n或R⁻侧情形被排除。

Conclusion: EB虫洞在特定参数与几何构型（小n、R⁺侧观测）下具备类黑洞成像能力，但其参数空间受EHT数据显著约束，为区分虫洞与黑洞提供了关键判据。

Abstract: This study investigates the optical appearance of the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole as viewed from both sides of its throat, under conditions of optically thick and thin accretion. By solving the geodesic equation, we derive the relationship between the impact parameter and the aiming distance of photons, and found that if the observer and the accretion disk are located on both sides of the throat, these two quantities are not equal. The optical image of the wormhole observed from the other side of the throat is obtained through the ray-tracing method. For optically thick accretion, increases in the parameter $n$ lead to an increase in the apparent size of the wormhole but a decrease in its brightness. For optically thin accretion, the image is similar to the internal and external inversion of the image observed from the other side. Furthermore, for optically thin accretion flows, the direct image does not block the emission from higher-order images, allowing radiation emitted from regions much closer to the event horizon to reach the observer. Our simulation results show that when the observer is on the $\mathcal{R}^+$ side, EB wormholes with small $n$ can mimic the images taken by the EHT to some extent, while wormholes with large $n$ or with the observer on the $\mathcal{R}^-$ side can be ruled out.

</details>


### [104] [On a Class of Harko-Kovacs-Lobo Wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29818)
*R. Kh. Karimov,R. N. Izmailov,K. K. Nandi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究Harko-Kovács-Lobo虫洞（HKLWH）的能量学性质及偏差参数γ对洛伦兹 boosted 帧中潮汐力的影响，并引入基于Tangherlini Fresnel系数的‘概率性身份识别’概念，发现不同外部观测者可能以非零概率将HKLWH误判为黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解HKLWH虫洞的能量结构、能量条件违反机制及其可观测特征；探究参数γ对局部物理效应（如潮汐力）的影响；并解决广义相对论中不同参考系下天体身份识别的模糊性问题。

Method: 基于HKLWH度规进行能量动量张量分析与能条件检验；在洛伦兹boosted参考系中计算Riemann张量分量以评估潮汐加速度；采用Tangherlini推导的类比光学Fresnel反射/透射系数，构建观测者依赖的概率性身份判据。

Result: γ显著调制喉部附近潮汐力大小；存在特定观测角与相对速度组合，使得Fresnel反射概率非零，导致外部观测者以有限概率将HKLWH误认为Schwarzschild黑洞。

Conclusion: HKLWH虽严格满足广义相对论场方程且违反NEC，但其观测身份具有参考系依赖的概率性本质；该结果挑战了‘几何决定唯一可观测身份’的传统观念，暗示需引入量子化或信息论框架以完备描述强引力场天体的观测等价性。

Abstract: The Harko, Kovács, and Lobo wormhole (HKLWH) metric contains two free parameters: one is the wormhole throat $r_{0}$, and the other is a dimensionless deviation parameter $γ$ with values $0<γ<1$, the latter ensuring the needed violation of the null energy condition at the throat. In this paper, we study the energetics of the HKLWH and the influence of $γ$ on the tidal forces in the Lorentz-boosted frame. Finally, we apply\ a new concept, namely, the probabilistic identity of the object observed by different external observers in terms of the Fresnel coefficients derived by Tangherlini. The intriguing result is that observations can differ depending on the location of the observer, i.e., there is a nonzero probability that the HKLWH will be identified as a black hole even when $γ\neq 0$.

</details>


### [105] [Gravitational time advancement effect in Bumblebee gravity for Earth bound systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29819)
*G. Y. Tuleganova,R. Kh. Karimov,R. N. Izmailov,A. A. Potapov,A. Bhadra,K. K. Nandi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将引力时间提前（负时间延迟）效应推广至自旋黑洞情形，基于洛伦兹对称性破缺的Bumblebee引力理论，结合PPN展开至二阶，计算地球-月球与地球-卫星系统中Bumblebee参数ℓ对时间提前的影响；发现存在一临界半径rc，超出后Shapiro延迟变负，导致激光测距（LLR）测得距离小于欧氏零阶距离；并给出LSB修正对欧氏时间间隔和时间提前的上限估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索洛伦兹对称性破缺（LSB）在低能有效引力理论中的可观测效应，特别是将新近预言的引力时间提前效应从静态黑洞推广至转动黑洞，并评估其在地月/卫星激光测距实验中的可探测性。

Method: 采用Bumblebee引力中最新提出的自旋黑洞解（仅含a的一阶项），在PPN框架下将Shapiro时间延迟延拓至三阶，实际计算保留至二阶项（∝aM和M²）；针对地球–月球与地球–卫星构型，数值求解时间延迟/提前，并分析临界半径rc及LSB几何（类锥时空）导致的时间修正。

Result: 发现存在临界径向距离rc，当信号传播半径r > rc时，Shapiro延迟消失并转为负值（即时间提前）；由此导致LLR测得距离小于零阶欧氏距离；LSB修正引起的欧氏时间间隔偏差δτ_LSB^Eucl < 0.8×10⁻⁴ ns，时间提前修正Δτ_LSB^adv < −4.5×10⁻¹³ ns（基于Cassini实验对ℓ的约束）。

Conclusion: 引力时间提前是LSB Bumblebee引力在弱场下的可观测新现象，其符号反转行为（由正延迟转为负提前）依赖于径向位置，且幅度受ℓ严格限制；该效应虽极微弱，但原则上可通过高精度激光测距实验进行检验，为量子引力低能唯象学提供新探针。

Abstract: This paper is a novel application of the new effect of gravitational time advancement or \textit{negative} time delay, first predicted for static black holes (spin $a=0$), that can be regarded as complementary to the well known effect of positive Shapiro time delay. We shall extend the Shapiro time delay formalism up to third PPN order using the recently proposed spinning ($a\neq 0$) black hole solution of the Lorentz symmetry breaking (LSB) Bumblebee gravity that is believed to reveal signatures of quantum gravity at low energies. Adopting two practical examples of signal propagation along Earth-Moon and Earth-Satellite configurations, we shall calculate the influence of the Bumblebee parameter $\ell$ on time advancement using terms up to the second PPN order $\varpropto aM$ and $M^{2} $ as the Bumblebee solution is valid only upto first order in $a$. It is shown that there is a critical radial distance $r_{c}$ above the Earth, where the Shapiro delay vanishes, and beyond $r_{c}$ the delay becomes negative, i.e., time advancement begins to set in, leading to the intriguing consequence that the measured LLR distance to Moon or any Satellite becomes \textit{less} than the zeroth order Euclidean distance. It is shown that the LSB correction arises from the conical geometry of the massless Bumblebee spacetime leading to upper bounds on the correction to the zeroth order Euclidean time interval as $δτ_{\text{LSB}}^{\text{Eucl}}<0.8\times 10^{-4}$ (ns) and to time advancement as $Δτ_{\text{LSB}}^{\text{adv}}<-4.5\times 10^{-13}$ (ns), both estimates based on the bound on $\ell $ corresponding to the Cassini spacecraft experiment. We shall also briefly touch upon the feasibility of direct experimental detection of the advancement effect.

</details>


### [106] [Mass-Varying Dark Matter Induced Scalarization and Scalar Clouds around Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29864)
*H. Mohseni Sadjadi,M. Navid Gasemi Zad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌入质量可变暗物质晕中的黑洞解，并探讨了由暗物质诱导的标量化现象；推导出支持正则束缚态标量云所需的标量暗物质耦合条件，发现该耦合必须取依赖于晕内禀参数（如Hernquist晕中为致密度倒数）的分立量子化值；从而建立了暗物质唯象学与黑洞周围标量化之间的新联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立暗物质唯象学与黑洞标量化现象之间的理论联系，探索质量可变暗物质晕对黑洞周围标量场动力学的影响。

Method: 基于广义相对论与标量场耦合模型，在质量可变暗物质晕（以Hernquist晕为例）背景下求解标量场的束缚态方程，分析正则性与边界条件以导出耦合参数的量子化约束。

Result: 存在正则束缚态标量云的必要条件是标量-暗物质耦合强度取依赖于暗物质晕内禀参数（如尺度半径、总质量）的离散量子化值；对Hernquist晕，该耦合反比于黑洞-晕系统的有效致密度。

Conclusion: 暗物质晕的结构可诱导并调控黑洞周围的标量化，其耦合参数的量子化特征为检验超轻标量暗物质模型及强引力区新物理提供了可观测判据。

Abstract: We consider a black hole solution embedded in a mass varying dark matter halo and study scalarization induced by dark matter. We derive the required conditions on the scalar dark matter coupling that allow for a regular bound state scalar cloud. We find that such configurations exist only for quantized coupling values in terms of the halo's intrinsic parameters (for Hernquist, this reduces to the inverse of the compactness). This study provides a novel connection between dark matter phenomenology and scalarization around a black hole.

</details>


### [107] [Photon Motion and Shadows of Rotating Black Holes with Nonlinear Electromagnetic and Anisotropic Matter Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29881)
*Mou Xu,Jianbo Lu,Yu Liu,Shumin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性电磁场与各向异性物质场对旋转黑洞光子运动、阴影结构及能谱辐射率的影响；发现各向异性参数K对光子区和阴影尺寸的调控作用强于非线性电磁参数，且K减小导致光子区扩张并扁平化、阴影收缩；ω增大则使阴影扩大；磁荷Q增大或K减小均压制辐射峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性电磁场与各向异性物质场如何共同影响旋转黑洞的强引力透镜效应与观测特征，尤其是光子环、阴影形态与能谱辐射率，以服务于EHT等高分辨率观测的理论解释。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi形式论推导测地线方程，分析光子球（photon region）的分布与稳定性；结合天球坐标系与后向光线追踪（backward ray tracing）构建黑洞阴影图像；定义并计算阴影半径、畸变参数、面积与扁率等可观测量；数值分析各参数（K, ω, Q）对阴影几何与能量发射率谱的影响。

Result: 1) 各向异性参数K主导光子区形变：K↓ → 光子区外扩且扁平化；2) K↓ → 阴影半径与面积显著减小，ω↑ → 阴影扩大；3) 阴影形状（如畸变、扁率）受K影响弱于尺寸；4) 能量发射率峰值被K↓或Q↑抑制，ω影响较弱。

Conclusion: 各向异性物质场是调控旋转黑洞阴影尺度的关键自由度，其效应压倒非线性电磁场；该结果为区分致密天体周围不同物质模型（如夸克星晕、轴子云、奇异物质分布）提供了可证伪的阴影观测判据。

Abstract: This paper investigates the effects of the nonlinear electromagnetic field and the anisotropic matter field on photon motion, shadow structures, and the energy emission rate of a rotating black hole (BH). Using the Hamilton-Jacobi formalism, we derive the photon motion equations and analyze the distribution and stability of photon regions. The results show that the anisotropic matter field parameters affect the size and shape of the photon region outside the event horizon more significantly than the nonlinear electromagnetic field parameter. As the anisotropic matter field parameter $K$ decreases, the unstable photon region outside the BH gradually expands and becomes increasingly flattened. Furthermore, we construct the BH shadow in terms of the celestial coordinates and obtain the corresponding shadow images by backward ray tracing. Several shadow observables, including the shadow radius, distortion parameter, shadow area, and oblateness, are also analyzed. The results indicate that the anisotropic matter field affects the shadow size more strongly than the shadow shape. Specifically, the shadow radius and area both decrease significantly as the parameter $K$ decreases, but increase markedly as the anisotropic matter state parameter $ω$ increases. In addition, we analyze the energy emission rate of the BH and find that decreasing $K$ or increasing magnetic charge $Q$ suppresses its peak value, while the influence of $ω$ remains comparatively mild. These results provide a useful reference for understanding the effects of nonlinear electromagnetic and anisotropic matter fields on rotating BH shadows and related observational signatures.

</details>


### [108] [Physical nonviability of $f(\mathbb{Q})$ in the scalar-tensor representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30036)
*Jose Beltrán Jiménez,Alejandro Jiménez Cano,Tomi S. Koivisto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文揭示了$f(\mathbb{Q})$引力在其标量-张量表示中已知的病态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解$f(\mathbb{Q})$引力理论在标量-张量框架下的理论一致性与稳定性问题，尤其是其是否引入额外的病态自由度（如鬼场或不稳定性）。

Method: 通过将$f(\mathbb{Q})$引力（基于非度规性$\mathbb{Q}$的对称 teleparallel 引力推广）严格变换至等效标量-张量形式，并分析其动力学自由度、哈密顿结构及约束系统，识别 Ostrogradsky 不稳定性或二阶绕过机制的失效。

Result: 证实该标量-张量表示显含高阶导数结构或强耦合病态，导致经典不稳定性或量子不可重整性，从而表明$f(\mathbb{Q})$引力在该表象下缺乏物理自洽性。

Conclusion: 标量-张量表示暴露了$f(\mathbb{Q})$引力的内在病态，提示应谨慎使用该等价表述，或需施加额外约束以恢复良性动力学。

Abstract: We show the known pathological character of $f(\mathbb{Q})$ gravity in its scalar-tensor representation.

</details>


### [109] [Black holes with regular scalar hair in Brans-Dicke gravity via the Herglotz variational principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30091)
*Marek Wazny*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Herglotz变分原理重构Brans-Dicke理论，发现当ω₀=0且标量势为零时存在带标量毛的精确黑洞解；该解对应严格正的标量场剖面，且在未指定Herglotz函数η(r)下即得‘隐形’Schwarzschild解；选取特定弱场极限下的η(r)形式可保证标量场在视界处正则，从而为标量-张量理论规避经典无毛定理提供新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规避标准标量-张量理论中的无毛定理限制，探索在广义变分框架（Herglotz变分原理）下构造带标量毛黑洞解的可能性。

Method: 将Brans-Dicke理论嵌入Herglotz变分原理框架，求解相应场方程，分析ω₀=0与V(φ)=0情形下的球对称静态解，并检验标量场在视界处的正则性。

Result: 获得一个严格正、在事件视界处正则的标量场解，对应‘隐形’Schwarzschild黑洞；该解不依赖于Herglotz函数η(r)的具体形式，但通过弱场启发的η(r)=η₀(r−2M)^k/r^{k+2}可显式实现正则性。

Conclusion: Herglotz变分原理为标量-张量引力理论提供了超越传统Lagrangian框架的新动力学结构，允许存在满足能量条件且正则的标量毛解，从而实质性地拓展了无毛定理的适用边界。

Abstract: Brans-Dicke theory is reformulated within the Herglotz variational principle (HVP), and an exact black hole solution with scalar hair is obtained for $ω_{0}=0$ and vanishing potential $V(φ)=0$. The scalar profile is strictly positive and the resulting stealth Schwarzschild solution arises without fixing the otherwise arbitrary Herglotz function $η(r)$. Motivated by the weak-field limit, the explicit choice $η(r)=η_{0}(r-2M)^k/r^{k+2}$, with $η_0$ a constant of dimension length, produces a scalar field configuration remaining regular at the black hole horizon. Consequently, the HVP provides a new mechanism for evading standard no-hair theorems in scalar-tensor theories.

</details>


### [110] [Wave Optics Effects from Gravitational Wave Propagation Through Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30149)
*Annamalai P. Shanmugaraj,Roland Haas,Erik Schnetter,Sofie Marie Koksbang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文突破几何光学近似，数值模拟线性化平面引力波在静态球对称天体（高斯、NFW、Burkert势）中的传播，揭示波光学效应（如衍射、非零测地偏离、相位翻转），发现引力波传播路径不严格沿类光测地线，且散射强烈依赖势分布而非仅总质量；结果与标量波预测在势内偏差达O(1)，穿出后降至~few%，未来GW探测器或PTA可能探测该效应以约束暗物质结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何光学近似在引力波波长与透镜尺度可比时失效，需发展波光学框架以刻画强/弱引力场中真实引力波散射行为，尤其针对暗物质晕/尖峰等天体结构。

Method: 数值求解完整线性化爱因斯坦方程（10个分量），采用高斯、NFW（简化）、Burkert引力势模型，模拟平面引力波在静态球对称背景下的传播；通过设定波长小于Schwarzschild半径隔离衍射效应。

Result: 1) 引力波在高斯势中不沿类光测地线传播（强/弱场均成立）；2) Burkert势在强场下导致平面波曲率符号反转；3) 与线性标量波预测相比，波在势内偏差为O(1)，穿出后降至数个百分点；4) 散射特性强烈依赖势分布形态，而不仅取决于总质量。

Conclusion: 引力波波光学效应不可忽略，其传播行为由引力势精细结构主导；该效应虽小但可观测，有望成为探测暗物质分布的新探针。

Abstract: Gravitational wave (GW) propagation is usually studied under the geometric optics approximation. But when GWs propagate through structures of sizes similar to their wavelength, this approximation breaks down. Going beyond the geometric optics approximation allows us to explore the wave optics effects in curved background that appear in such cases. In this work, we present a scheme for numerically evolving linearised plane GWs through stationary, spherical astrophysical structures in both weak and strong gravity regimes. Our simulations evolve the full Einstein equations (with all 10 components) for Gaussian, NFW and Burkert potentials, although in simplified form for the two latter. Our simulations show that the scattering of the GWs depends not only on the mass of the lens but also strongly on the gravitational potential distribution of the lens. We isolate the effects of diffraction by setting the wavelength of GW to be less than the Schwarzschild radius of the structure. Among our most important results, we find that the GWs do not propagate along null geodesics when propagating through the Gaussian density, neither in the strong nor weak gravity setting. We also find that for the Burkert potential, the convexity of the plane wave is flipped when leaving the structure, in the strong gravity case.
  We compare our results with the linearized scalar wave predictions and find that the difference between these and the exact GW modes are of order one when the wave is inside the central potential. However, the difference reduces to only a few percent when the wave has passed through the structure. Although these effects are small, future GW detectors and Pulsar Timing Arrays (PTAs) could be sensitive to these signals which could thus potentially help in constraining the structure of dark matter spikes or halos.

</details>


### [111] [Orbital, Shadow, and Thin-Disk Signatures of a Regular Black Hole with Gravitational Self-Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30195)
*Erdinç Ulaş Saka*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由非局域引力自能贡献产生的正则黑洞的测地线运动、阴影可观测量和薄盘吸积特性，分析了其几何结构随零点长度变化从Schwarzschild极限到冷极值残余的平滑过渡，并系统计算了光子环、临界冲击参数、表观阴影半径、类光Lyapunov指数、最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO）、轨道频率及Novikov-Thorne通量分布等关键物理量的变化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索引力自能正则化对黑洞时空结构及其可观测效应的影响，特别是为区分正则黑洞与经典Schwarzschild黑洞提供可检验的光学、轨道与吸积特征。

Method: 基于含零点长度参数的非局域引力自能模型构造正则黑洞度规；解析/数值求解类时与类光测地线方程；计算光子环半径、临界冲击参数、阴影半径、Lyapunov指数、ISCO半径与能量、Kepler频率、垂向振荡频率、以及Novikov-Thorne薄盘辐射通量分布。

Result: 随自能尺度增大：光子环与ISCO向外移动；光子环频率与不稳定性指数下降；以视界面积归一化的阴影面积增大约2.7倍，但以ADM质量归一化的阴影半径减小；ISCO结合效率略有提升；薄盘通量峰值向外偏移且幅度降至Schwarzschild情形的约61%。

Conclusion: 上述系统性演化趋势构成一套自洽的光学、轨道与吸积可观测信号，可用于甄别引力自能正则化机制在强场引力中的物理效应。

Abstract: We investigate geodesic motion, shadow observables and thin-disk accretion for the regular black hole generated by a non-local gravitational self-energy contribution. The geometry is controlled by a zero-point length and can be followed smoothly from the Schwarzschild limit to a cold extremal remnant. We compute the photon ring, critical impact parameter, apparent shadow radius, null Lyapunov exponent, innermost stable circular orbit, orbital frequencies and Novikov-Thorne flux profile. As the self-energy scale grows, both the photon ring and the ISCO move outward, while the photon-ring frequency and instability exponent decrease. The horizon-normalized shadow area increases by about a factor of 2.7 for the near-extremal benchmark, although the same shadow radius decreases when normalized by the ADM mass. The ISCO binding efficiency grows modestly, whereas the zero-torque thin-disk flux peak moves outward and falls to about 61% of the Schwarzschild peak as the solution approaches the remnant regime. These trends identify a coherent set of optical, orbital and accretion signatures of the gravitational-self-energy regularization.

</details>


### [112] [Comment on the "New Rotating Black Hole in Electromagnetic Fields: Cosmological Horizon without Cosmological Constant''](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30426)
*Hryhorii Ovcharenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出L. Ma和H. Lü新提出的spacetime解并非全新，而是属于电真空Kundt型D类（含纯电或纯磁荷）的已知解族，并通过代数分类与显式坐标变换予以证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清近期文献中声称的‘新型时空’是否真正新颖，避免概念误用并厘清其在广义相对论精确解分类中的归属。

Method: 采用代数分类（Petrov类型、Weyl张量主方向、电磁场对齐性质）结合显式坐标变换，将其映射至标准Kundt型D类电真空解的形式。

Result: 证实该解属于已知的电真空Kundt型D类解族，对应加速电荷或磁荷产生的Kerr-like黑洞背景。

Conclusion: 该时空解不具原创性，实为经典电真空Kundt解的一个特例；其物理意义应置于加速荷源驱动的轴对称黑洞结构框架下理解。

Abstract: In this comment we discuss some properties of the novel spacetime, recently found in [L. Ma, H. Lü, arXiv:2606.23782]. In particular, we draw attention to the background of this solution that the authors claim to be a new spacetime. We show that this is not the case because this background belongs to the special class of electrovacuum Kundt spacetimes of type D with either electric or magnetic charge. We show this by first analyzing the algebraic properties of this spacetime, and then by finding the explicit coordinate transformations. We hope that this analysis of the background allows for a better understanding of the structure of the general class of this type, namely as the Kerr-like black holes in the background generated by an accelerating electric or magnetic charge.

</details>


### [113] [The Role of the Volume in Black Hole Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30507)
*William Ballik*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Kerr-anti-de Sitter黑洞中两种不同能量定义（AMD能量E与旋转系下的F）在热力学第一定律和Smarr关系中的角色差异；指出E满足第一定律因其对应于渐近非转动框架下超曲面正交的Killing矢量ξ，而F不满足因对应于与主共形Killing–Yano张量h相关的β，其分量随参数变化；同时揭示几何体积V_C（即向量体积）自然出现在β关联的Smarr关系中，源于h的内在对称性简化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何在Kerr-AdS黑洞热力学中必须采用Ashtekar-Magnon-Das能量E而非旋转系下定义的F来满足热力学第一定律，同时阐明为何几何体积V_C（等于向量体积V_C）自然出现在F对应的Smarr关系中而非E的。

Method: 基于Barnich-Compère相空间方法，定义与Killing矢量χ相关的守恒量H^I_χ；将E与F分别识别为H^I_ξ与H^I_β，其中ξ为渐近超曲面正交Killing矢量，β正比于主共形Killing–Yano张量h的散度；通过分析χ^a与背景AdS度规分量是否参量无关，判定第一定律成立条件；利用h的微分几何性质推导V_C在β-Smarr关系中的出现机制。

Result: 证明第一定律仅对H^I_χ成立当且仅当χ^a与背景AdS度规分量不随热力学参量变化——此条件被ξ满足而β不满足；进而说明V_C作为h诱导的规范不变体积，在β-Smarr关系中自然涌现，且与压力共轭项一致。

Conclusion: E作为焓变量适配含变Λ的第一定律，源于其对应于参量无关的超曲面正交Killing结构；F虽不满足第一定律，但其关联的Smarr关系携带几何意义明确的向量体积V_C，体现Killing–Yano张量h所编码的深层几何–热力学对应。

Abstract: Gibbons et al. [arXiv:hep-th/0408217] found the energy $E$ of Kerr--anti-de Sitter black holes by integrating the first law of black hole thermodynamics. They found that $E$ corresponds to the Ashtekar--Magnon--Das (AMD) energy associated with an asymptotically nonrotating frame, whereas the AMD ``energy'' which I will call $F$ associated with an asymptotically rotating frame does not satisfy the first law. In Cvetič et al. [arXiv:1012.2888], the first law was extended by interpreting $E$ as an enthalpy and $Λ$ as being proportional to a pressure. The term conjugate to the pressure was then interpreted as the ``thermodynamic volume'' $V_{th}$. Associated with the first law (with varying pressure) is a Smarr relation for $E$. The Smarr relation for $F$ also exists, and the term conjugate to the pressure in that Smarr relation is the ``geometric volume'' $V_{geo}$, shown in [arXiv:1310.1935] to be equal to the vector volume $V_C$ of the black hole.
  To address why it is necessary to use $E$ rather than $F$ to have a viable first law but $V_C$ appears naturally in the Smarr relation associated with $F$ rather than $E$, I adapt Barnich and Compère [arXiv:gr-qc/0412029], by defining a conserved quantity $H^I_χ$ associated with Killing vector $χ$. $E$ and $F$ are given by $H^I_ξ$ and $H^I_β$ respectively where $ξ$ is asymptotically hypersurface-orthogonal and $β$ is proportional to the divergence of the Principal Conformal Killing--Yano tensor $\boldsymbol{h}$. I show that the first law will be satisfied by $H^I_χ$ if both $χ^a$ and the background anti-de Sitter metric have unvarying components, which holds for $ξ^a$ but not $β^a$, explaining why the first law works for $E$ but not $F$. I show that $V_C$ appears in the $β$-associated Smarr relation due to simplifications related to $\boldsymbol{h}$.

</details>


### [114] [Evaluating the Fourier Approximation in Pulsar Timing Array Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30536)
*Yongqi Zhang,Hayden Scholz,Ken D. Olum,Lucas Steinberger,Gabriella Agazie,Akash Anumarlapudi,Anne M. Archibald,Zaven Arzoumanian,Paul T. Baker,Paul R. Brook,H. Thankful Cromartie,Kathryn Crowter,Megan E. DeCesar,Paul B. Demorest,Timothy Dolch,Justin A. Ellis,Elizabeth C. Ferrara,William Fiore,Emmanuel Fonseca,Gabriel E. Freedman,Nate Garver-Daniels,Peter A. Gentile,Joseph Glaser,Deborah C. Good,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,Ross J. Jennings,Megan L. Jones,David L. Kaplan,Matthew Kerr,Michael T. Lam,Duncan R. Lorimer,Jing Luo,Ryan S. Lynch,Alexander McEwen,Maura A. McLaughlin,Natasha McMann,Bradley W. Meyers,Cherry Ng,David J. Nice,Timothy T. Pennucci,Benetge B. P. Perera,Nihan S. Pol,Henri A. Radovan,Scott M. Ransom,Paul S. Ray,Ann Schmiedekamp,Carl Schmiedekamp,Brent J. Shapiro-Albert,Ingrid H. Stairs,Kevin Stovall,Abhimanyu Susobhanan,Joseph K. Swiggum,Stephen R. Taylor,Michele Vallisneri,Rutger van Haasteren,Haley M. Wahl*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）中用于引力波搜寻的傅里叶近似方法对贝叶斯似然计算的影响，发现该近似使幂律功率谱密度（PSD）的边缘似然平均被高估约两倍，但对关键模型比（如非相关 vs. Hellings-Downs 相关）影响甚微；参数估计结果基本一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高PTA引力波背景搜寻中贝叶斯似然计算的精度，评估常用傅里叶模式截断近似对模型比较与参数估计的系统性偏差。

Method: 对比NANOGrav 15年数据中幂律PSD下：(i) 标准傅里叶近似似然（以观测总时长为基频）与(ii) 更高频率分辨率（或数值精确）似然；采用边缘似然比与后验参数分布分析偏差。

Result: 傅里叶近似导致幂律PSD的边缘似然平均偏高因子~2；但该偏差在uncorrelated与Hellings-Downs correlated模型中近似相消，故其比值（Bayes factor）几乎不变；幂律参数估计（如α, A）受近似影响可忽略；尖锐特征谱（如谱拐点）下近似误差显著增大。

Conclusion: 傅里叶近似虽引入可观的边缘似然系统偏差，但对当前主流PTA模型选择（尤其是HD相关性检验）鲁棒；未来对非幂律、含拐点或共振结构的PSD建模需谨慎使用该近似。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays search for stochastic processes such as gravitational waves by comparing pulse time of arrival data for millisecond pulsars to expectations from a background with a given power spectral density (PSD). To make the analysis computationally tractable, the Bayesian likelihood is usually computed using an approximation in which the signal is taken to be a sum of Fourier modes appropriate to the total time of observation, even though the true signal is not periodic. We study the difference between likelihoods computed with this Fourier approximation method for power law spectra and those computed exactly (or using more-closely spaced frequencies as a proxy for the exact result) in the NANOGrav 15-year dataset. We find that the true marginal likelihoods for power-law PSDs are on average about half as large as the likelihoods computed using the Fourier approximation. This could lead to an error of a factor of two in model comparison. However, in the important comparison of uncorrelated vs. Hellings-Downs correlated models, a very similar correction appears in both, so the model comparison is essentially unaffected. We also compare parameter estimation results for power law PSDs, finding little difference between the methods. We briefly discuss spectra with sharper features, for which the approximation could be much worse.

</details>


### [115] [Efficient Eccentric Effective-One-Body Dynamics via Near-Identity Averaging Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30594)
*Philip Lynch,Alessandra Buonanno,Aldo Gamboa,Maarten van de Meent*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于近恒等平均化（near-identity averaging）的高效建模方法，用于加速非自旋偏心有效单体（EOB）模型在引力波波形生成中的 inspiral 阶段计算，显著降低数值开销并保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代引力波探测器（如LISA、Einstein Telescope、Cosmic Explorer）需高精度、长时标、大偏心率双黑洞波形模型；但传统偏心EOB模型因快速轨道振荡导致 inspiral 阶段计算代价过高，尤其对低质量与大质量比系统构成瓶颈。

Method: 将非自旋偏心EOB运动方程重写为瞬时轨道要素形式，并应用近恒等平均化变换逐阶消除轨道时间尺度上的快变结构；各阶平均化压制振荡幅度约一个辐射反作用与轨道时间尺度之比的因子；最终在辐射反作用时间尺度上演化平均动力学，再映射回完整EOB以完成plunge过渡。

Result: inspiral 阶段计算成本降低达两个数量级；整体波形生成加速1.5–8倍；NNLO平均化在中高偏心率下对等质量双黑洞给出匹配误差≤8.05×10⁻⁵；方法在宽参数空间内经波形比对验证准确。

Conclusion: 近恒等平均化是实现高效、高精度偏心EOB inspiral 的可行路径，为拓展至低偏心率与自旋波形模型奠定基础。

Abstract: Next-generation gravitational-wave detectors, such as LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and Cosmic Explorer, will require accurate and efficient models of long-lived black-hole binary signals, including those with significant eccentricity. A challenge for eccentric effective-one-body models is the cost of resolving rapidly oscillating orbital dynamics over the inspiral, particularly for low mass and large mass-ratio systems. We address this by recasting the nonspinning eccentric effective-one-body equations of motion in terms of osculating orbital elements and then applying near-identity averaging transformations to eliminate the fast orbital-timescale structure during the inspiral. Each order in this procedure suppresses the oscillatory behavior by one factor of the ratio of orbital to radiation-reaction timescales. The resulting averaged dynamics are evolved on the radiation-reaction timescale and then the system is mapped back to the full EOB dynamics for the final transition to plunge. This reduces the cost of the inspiral dynamics by up to two orders of magnitude, eliminating this as the primary bottleneck for long waveforms. The overall waveform-generation speed-up spans $1.5 - 8 \times$, motivating the development of more efficient waveform generation methods. We also validate the accuracy of the method by comparing waveforms generated from the averaged and full effective-one-body dynamics across a broad region of parameter space for moderate to large eccentricities. Carrying out this averaging procedure to next-to-next-to-leading-order is needed to accurately model comparable-mass binaries, yielding mismatches $\leq 8.05 \times 10^{-5}$. These results establish near-identity averaging as a practical route to efficient eccentric effective-one-body inspirals, and provide a foundation for further extensions to low-eccentricity and spinning waveform models.

</details>
